Title: The Spiritual Guide which disentangles the Soul, and brings it by the Inward Way to the getting of Perfect Contemplation and the Rich Treasure of Internal Peace; with a short Treatise concerning Daily Communion, by the same Author; translated from the Italian copy
Creator: Miguel de Molinos; translator not named
Publisher: England: printed for Tho. Fabian (imprint as the host's catalogue record gives it; the title page is not legible in the scan)
Publication Date: 1688
Rights: Public Domain (published 1688, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item bim_early-english-books-1641-1700_the-spiritual-guide-whic_molinos-miguel-de_1688 (https://archive.org/details/bim_early-english-books-1641-1700_the-spiritual-guide-whic_molinos-miguel-de_1688), the item's own bim_early-english-books-1641-1700_the-spiritual-guide-whic_molinos-miguel-de_1688_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Internet Archive; host metadata date: 1688. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 35bd50ae4075b471b42f0dbb6c8d9089f2f927d730744f350454ced8ea5f8137.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Early English Books microfilm scan contributed to the Internet Archive; the title page is badly garbled and the host notes tight binding with slight loss of text. The body is usable, with the long s kept.
Content note: The first English translation, published the year after the condemnation: the translator's preface on the affair, the approbations, the three books of the Guide and the Treatise of daily communion.

PREFACE. 


HE following Treatiſe was approved of 
at Rome by Fryer Peter Damian, & 

' Diſcalceate Carmelite, Viſitor General 
and Reader of Theo!ogy in the Convent 

of $. Mary della Scala, in the Year 167 5, mhenthe
